Player_Black (to Proxy):

Only my last two lines are usable: the legal-move list you pasted, and the position from the first board. Everything else in my earlier messages was garbled and unreliable, so I'm working from that board alone.

The white king on f4 has just one flight square, g5. Every other square is covered:
- e3, f3 and g3 are covered by my rook.
- e4 and g4 are covered by the f5 pawn.
- e5 is covered by the d6 pawn.
- f5 is covered by the e6 pawn.

Because of that, a quiet move such as h6 would be stalemate, since White has only a king. I need to give check instead.

My plan is Rf3+, which is forced: the king can't take on f3 because the knight guards it, so Kg5 is the only reply. After that:
- h6+ Kh5 Rh3# is mate.
- h6+ Kh4 Ng6+ Kh5 Rh3# is also mate.
